# Donovan — animation node chains (appendix of the character-data map)

GENERATED by `tools/charmap_md.py --anim` from the map JSON. One block per chain; one line per node: `addr` (vs2) · `off` (region offset) · dur · flags · hb8 (the hitbox FAMILY: vuln/push ids) · hbA (hbA>>8 = the ATTACK record, shown in `atk rec`) · shadow seq · sfx idx · link · `!` if ours differs (with the attribution). Frame data per chain is derived from the attack nodes (phase 2).
